errors cannot refund a paid-start slot.\\r\\ncreate or replace function public.reserve_ingestion_attempt(p_target_id uuid, p_owner_id uuid)\\r\\nreturns jsonb language plpgsql security definer set search_path = '' as $$\\r\\ndeclare target public.analysis_targets; attempt public.ingestion_attempts;\\r\\nbegin\\r\\n  select * into target from public.analysis_targets where id = p_target_id and owner_id = p_owner_id for update;\\r\\n  if not found then raise exception 'Target unavailable' using errcode = 'P0002'; end if;\\r\\n  select * into attempt from public.ingestion_attempts where analysis_target_id = p_target_id\\r\\n    and (status in ('processing', 'succeeded') or error_code in ('SUBMISSION_UNCERTAIN', 'RESULT_WINDOW_EXPIRED'))\\r\\n    order by (status = 'succeeded') desc, started_at desc, id desc limit 1;\\r\\n  if found then return jsonb_build_object('attempt', to_jsonb(attempt), 'created', false); end if;\\r\\n  -- Serialize distinct-target reservations for this owner, not just starts on one target.\\r\\n  perform pg_catalog.pg_advisory_xact_lock(pg_catalog.hashtextextended('reviewlens:paid-start:' || p_owner_id::text, 0));\\r\\n  if (select count(*) from public.ingestion_attempts where owner_id = p_owner_id\\r\\n      and started_at > clock_timestamp() - interval '24 hours') >= 5 then\\r\\n    raise exception 'INGESTION_START_LIMIT' using errcode = 'P0001';\\r\\n  end if;\\r\\n  insert into public.ingestion_attempts(analysis_target_id, owner_id, source_url, submitted_query, query_kind, expected_place_id)\\r\\n  values (target.id, target.owner_id, target.source_url, coalesce(btrim(target.google_place_id), target.source_url),\\r\\n    case when target.google_place_id is null then 'source_url' else 'place_id' end, btrim(target.google_place_id))\\r\\n  returning * into attempt;\\r\\n  return jsonb_build_object('attempt', to_jsonb(attempt), 'created', true);\\r\\nend $$;\\r\\n\\r\\nrevoke all on function public.reserve_ingestion_attempt(uuid, uuid) from public, anon, authenticated;\\r\\ngrant execute on function public.reserve_ingestion_attempt(uuid, uuid) to service_role;\\r\\nimport type { SupabaseClient } from \\\"@supabase/supabase-js\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nimport type { SupportedReviewPlatform } from \\\"@/lib/validation/analysis-target\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type ReviewPlatform = SupportedReviewPlatform;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface AnalysisTarget {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  ownerId: string;\\r\\n  name: string;\\r\\n  platform: ReviewPlatform;\\r\\n  sourceUrl: string;\\r\\n  /** Google place ID resolved during ingestion (S1-020); null until then. */\\r\\n  googlePlaceId: string | null;\\r\\n  createdAt: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Already-validated target fields. Ownership is never part of this input. */\\r\\nexport interface NewAnalysisTarget {\\r\\n  name: string;\\r\\n  platform: ReviewPlatform;\\r\\n  sourceUrl: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface AnalysisTargetRow {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  owner_id: string;\\r\\n  name: string;\\r\\n  platform: ReviewPlatform;\\r\\n  source_url: string;\\r\\n  google_place_id: string | null;\\r\\n  created_at: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst TABLE = \\\"analysis_targets\\\";\\r\\nconst COLUMNS = \\\"id, owner_id, name, platform, source_url, google_place_id, created_at\\\";\\r\\nconst UUID_PATTERN = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport class AnalysisTargetPersistenceError extends Error {\\r\\n  readonly code = \\\"ANALYSIS_TARGET_PERSISTENCE_FAILED\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n  constructor(operation: \\\"create\\\" | \\\"get\\\" | \\\"list\\\", options?: { cause?: unknown }) {\\r\\n    // The message is safe to show; database details stay on `cause` for server-side logging.\\r\\n    super(`Could not ${operation} the analysis target.`, options);\\r\\n    this.name = \\\"AnalysisTargetPersistenceError\\\";\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction toAnalysisTarget(row: AnalysisTargetRow): AnalysisTarget {\\r\\n  return {\\r\\n    id: row.id,\\r\\n    ownerId: row.owner_id,\\r\\n    name: row.name,\\r\\n    platform: row.platform,\\r\\n    sourceUrl: row.source_url,\\r\\n    googlePlaceId: row.google_place_id,\\r\\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\\r\\n  };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Persists a new AnalysisTarget owned by `ownerId`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * `ownerId` must come from the verified session (S1-011), never from request input. The client\\r\\n * must be a user-scoped Supabase client so Row Level Security also checks ownership.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function createAnalysisTarget(\\r\\n  client: SupabaseClient,\\r\\n  ownerId: string,\\r\\n  input: NewAnalysisTarget,\\r\\n): Promise<AnalysisTarget> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await client\\r\\n    .from(TABLE)\\r\\n    .insert({\\r\\n      owner_id: ownerId,\\r\\n      name: input.name,\\r\\n      platform: input.platform,\\r\\n      source_url: input.sourceUrl,\\r\\n    })\\r\\n    .select(COLUMNS)\\r\\n    .single<AnalysisTargetRow>();\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error || !data) {\\r\\n    throw new AnalysisTargetPersistenceError(\\\"create\\\", { cause: error });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return toAnalysisTarget(data);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Returns the target with `targetId` if it belongs to `ownerId`, otherwise `null`.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Missing and foreign targets both return `null` so callers can respond with 404 without\\r\\n * revealing that another user's target exists.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function getAnalysisTargetById(\\r\\n  client: SupabaseClient,\\r\\n  ownerId: string,\\r\\n  targetId: string,\\r\\n): Promise<AnalysisTarget | null> {\\r\\n  if (!UUID_PATTERN.test(targetId)) {\\r\\n    return null;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await client\\r\\n    .from(TABLE)\\r\\n    .select(COLUMNS)\\r\\n    .eq(\\\"id\\\", targetId)\\r\\n    .eq(\\\"owner_id\\\", ownerId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle<AnalysisTargetRow>();\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (error) {\\r\\n    throw new AnalysisTargetPersistenceError(\\\"get\\\", { cause: error });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return data ? toAnalysisTarget(data) : null;\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport type { SupabaseClient } from \\\"@supabase/supabase-js\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { type AnalysisTarget, getAnalysisTargetById } from \\\"./analysis-target-repository\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Thrown when the target does not exist OR belongs to another user.\\r\\n * The two cases are deliberately indistinguishable (maps to HTTP 404) so\\r\\n * responses never reveal another user's private resource.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport class TargetNotFoundError extends Error {\\r\\n  readonly code = \\\"TARGET_NOT_FOUND\\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n  constructor() {\\r\\n    super(\\\"Analysis target not found.\\\");\\r\\n    this.name = \\\"TargetNotFoundError\\\";\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * S1-013: parent-ownership guard for every child operation on an AnalysisTarget (reviews,\\r\\n * ingestion). Call it before reading the request body or doing any child work.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Returns the target only if it belongs to `userId`. Missing, foreign, and malformed ids all throw\\r\\n * the same `TargetNotFoundError`. `userId` must come from `requireApiUser()`, never from request\\r\\n * input (S1-BR-010), and `client` must be the The UI explicitly distinguishes this cited subset from the complete saved review dataset.\\n\\nReviewLens currently uses bounded complete-dataset context rather than question-specific retrieval. For S3-014, “supporting reviews” therefore means the unique reviews cited by the validated answer; this does not claim to discover every potentially relevant review.\\n\\n## Changes\\n\\n- Show up to three numbered supporting reviews inline.\\n- For larger cited sets, provide a native **View all N supporting reviews** disclosure containing the full cited set in citation order. The full list intentionally repeats the inline preview and explains that behavior.\\n- Preserve every citation's numbering, persisted text/rating/date and safe unavailable-record fallback.\\n- Reuse active-target citation resolution and escaped text rendering; opening the disclosure performs no network request or paid model call.\\n- Add component/integration coverage for membership, ordering, unavailable records, safe markup, account/target changes, preserved-page cleanup and subsequent questions.\\n- Add a dedicated synthetic browser account with seven saved fixture reviews, of which six are cited. Existing fixture accounts' counts remain unchanged.\\n- Update UI standards and retain the original available Codex dialogue/tool records with privacy redactions and an explicit cutoff.\\n\\nNo API, database schema, migration, dependency, model, prompt, quota, production configuration or review-selection contract changed.\\n\\n## Requirements and boundaries\\n\\n- **S3-BR-020:** Users can inspect the full cited set when it exceeds the inline preview.\\n- **S3-BR-021:** Existing server authorization and active-target resolution remain authoritative. Foreign-target evidence is unavailable, never fetched through a broader fallback.\\n- **S3-BR-022:** Evidence fields come from stored Review objects; unavailable data is not invented; markup remains inert.\\n- **S3-BR-023:** The cited subset complements the complete review browser already merged under S3-015.\\n- **S3-BR-024:** Expansion remains scoped to the active target and does not query or mutate the persisted dataset.\\n\\nThis is a read-only presentation change, not a replacement for backend ownership/RLS or semantic-grounding evaluation.\\n\\n## Verification\\n\\nBaseline: main `24f083fb`. Node 24.21.0; Chromium; local fixture services only.\\n\\n| Check | Actual result |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| Focused component/integration and inherited citation tests | 24/24 passed |\\n| Full Jest with coverage | 112 suites / 2,052 tests passed; no skips |\\n| Changed-line coverage | 100% — 54/54 source lines; 4/4 changed branch outcomes |\\n| Overall line/statement coverage | 96.10% |\\n| Formatting, lint, explicit typecheck, migration filename checks | Passed |\\n| Production build | Passed; 12/12 static pages |\\n| New supporting-review browser scenario | Passed in development and production |\\n| Keyboard / responsive checks | Enter/Space open/close, 375px layout, full-set membership and reload cleanup passed |\\n| Full production authentication browser suite | **42 passed / 2 failed**, detailed below |\\n\\nMutation checks caught all three deliberate faults: truncated expanded evidence (5 test failures), missing explorer (9 failures), and removed active-target resolution (1 failure). All temporary mutations were restored.\\n\\nValidation used isolated checkouts without the user's `.env.local`, disabled paid features and a recording/mocked model boundary. A temporary Turbopack root setting accommodated linked dependencies in validation and was restored; it is not in this PR. No live Supabase, OpenAI or Outscraper acceptance is claimed.\\n\\n### Known baseline browser failures\\n\\nTwo existing scenarios in `tests/e2e/auth/analysis-targets.spec.ts` (lines 99 and 177) expect the old `Target created. You can now start ingestion.` banner. Snapshots show the created workspace opened successfully instead.\\n\\n**Both failures were independently reproduced on a separate unchanged-main `24f083fb` production build without this PR's source or fixture changes.** They predate S3-014. Their assertions were not weakened, skipped or repaired in this story. The full browser suite is not being reported as green.\\n\\n## Integration\\n\\n- S3-015 / #129 is already included in the baseline.\\n- S3-013 / #127 changes `ReviewQuestions.tsx` and `citations.ts`; this PR leaves those files and its changed tests alone.\\n- The 11 new tests passed in a temporary combination with S3-013 `fa106e14` and main. Its later `70b026c` follow-up changes test synchronization/transcript only.\\n- Recheck against S3-013's final approved head. Its stricter parser also requires remaining legacy browser stubs to supply valid citations; the new S3-014 browser response already does.\\n- Coordinate future filter/history work so browsing filters never silently broaden or replace an answer's cited evidence.\\n\\n## Manual verification\\n\\n1. Open an owned analysis with saved reviews and obtain a grounded answer.\\n2. For more than three citations, open **View all N supporting reviews** and inspect the later cited records and unchanged numbering.\\n3. Confirm these are labeled as cited evidence, not the entire dataset. For three or fewer citations, all are already inline.\\n4. Switch account/target, submit another question, or obtain a non-answer: old evidence must disappear.\\n5. Use the deterministic browser test for a guaranteed six-citation demo; a live model is not guaranteed to cite more than three reviews.\\n\\n```powershell\\nnpx.cmd playwright test --config=playwright.auth.config.ts tests/e2e/auth/supporting-reviews.spec.ts\\n```\\n\\nDesktop/mobile screenshots are retained locally in the isolated validation checkout's ignored `test-results` folder; no screenshots, traces, credentials or generated test artifacts are committed.\\n\\n## Review and remaining acceptance\\n\\nDraft intentionally: current-head CI and independent functional/security review are pending; the known baseline browser failures must stay tracked. Final applicable public-deployment/manual acceptance is not yet recorded. Keep issue #120 open until final acceptance and merge. Do not merge automatically or bypass protected-branch checks.\\n\\n## Evidence and AI assistance\\n\\nAI assistance: **OpenAI Codex**. No sub-agents were used.\\n\\n- [Implementation, canonical-rule mapping and test evidence](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/blob/1dfe31a5d68b3eed25c80df4131d5c569242a68f/docs/s3-014-supporting-reviews.md)\\n- [Original available task and follow-up transcript](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/blob/1dfe31a5d68b3eed25c80df4131d5c569242a68f/ai-transcripts/2026-11-25_shawn_S3-014_codex.md)\\n\",\"state\":null,\"user\":null,\"assignees\":null,\"labels\":null,\"milestone\":null,\"state_reason\":null,\"comments\":null,\"created_at\":null,\"updated_at\":null,\"closed_at\":null,\"display_url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/133\",\"display_title\":\"S3-014: Explore the complete cited review set\"},{\"id\":null,\"url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/131\",\"title\":\"Increase per-account Q&A quota from 5 to 30\",\"issue_number\":131,\"body\":\"Closes #130\\n\\n## Summary and motivation\\n\\nRaises the durable per-account Review Q&A allowance from five to 30 attempts in a rolling 24-hour window so authenticated users can analyze multiple businesses. The rolling window and existing failure-charging policy are unchanged.\\n\\n## Backend and frontend changes\\n\\n- Changes `MAX_QUESTIONS_PER_24_HOURS` from 5 to 30.\\n- Accepts database remaining-quota values from 0 through 29 and fails closed for 30, negative, fractional, or malformed values.\\n- Updates active server, client, and accessible UI copy to communicate: “Each account can make 30 attempts within 24 hours. Failed answer-service attempts also count.”\\n- Preserves application error codes, HTTP response conventions, visual design, and accessibility.\\n\\n## Database migration\\n\\nAdds `20261125000000_increase_review_question_quota.sql`, which replaces only the body of `public.reserve_review_question()` without replacing its table or existing reservations:\\n\\n- Attempts 1–30 are accepted and attempt 31 is denied.\\n- The first reservation returns 29 remaining; the thirtieth returns 0.\\n- Active reservations continue to expire independently after 24 hours.\\n- A maintenance comment documents that the SQL limit must match `MAX_QUESTIONS_PER_24_HOURS`; executable SQL is unchanged by the review follow-up.\\n\\nThe historical migration `20261115043442_limit_paid_review_questions.sql` is unchanged.\\n\\n## Security and cost-control safeguards\\n\\nThe migration retains authenticated identity from `auth.uid()`, account-wide enforcement across all AnalysisTargets, transaction-level advisory locking, atomic inserts, existing rows, rolling expiration, `SECURITY DEFINER` with an empty search path, restricted table access, RLS, and authenticated-only RPC execution. Failed provider attempts remain charged.\\n\\nThe OpenAI provider/model, API keys, per-question token budget, review-context limits, system prompt, scope guards, citation validation, authentication, ownership, cross-user isolation, ingestion, Q&A history, and provider-failure handling are unchanged.\\n\\nThe maximum permitted usage is six times higher. Actual API cost remains dependent on actual attempts and token usage. The per-account quota is not an application-wide spending cap; total exposure scales with account count.\\n\\nA separate, uncreated operational follow-up is proposed for Deep or the authorized OpenAI project owner: **Configure OpenAI project spending limits and alerts before public deployment**. It should cover a team-approved monthly budget, provider hard-limit enforcement where available, alerts, deployed-key project verification, monitoring and emergency `QA_ENABLED=false` procedures, and possible delayed enforcement or spending overrun. This PR does not access billing or add an application-wide quota.\\n\\n## Test evidence\\n\\n- Focused question-quota Jest suite: 1 suite, 17 tests passed, including rejection of both `1.5` and `30.5`.\\n- `npm run format:check`: passed.\\n- `npm run check:migrations`: passed; migration filenames and versions are valid and unique.\\n- `npm run lint`: passed.\\n- `npm run typecheck`: passed; Next.js route types generated.\\n- `npm test`: 107 suites, 2,033 tests passed.\\n- `npm run build`: passed with Next.js 16.4.6.\\n- Full instrumented Jest: 107 suites, 2,033 tests passed; 95.55% line coverage (9,799/10,255).\\n- Changed-code coverage: 100% overall (4/4 changed source lines) and 100% in `src/services/qa/**` (2/2).\\n- No live OpenAI, Outscraper, or hosted Supabase request was made.\\n\\nThe Playwright quota-copy expectation was updated in the original implementation but was not run against a configured authenticated environment in this session.\\n\\n## Deployment coordination\\n\\nApplication/database versions must be coordinated because the old application rejects new remaining values 5–29 after the database has already consumed a reservation. The updated application against the old database remains compatible but still enforces the old five-attempt limit.\\n\\nSafe sequence:\\n\\n1. Set `QA_ENABLED=false` in the hosting provider’s environment settings, deploy or redeploy the application with that environment, and confirm Q&A is disabled before applying the database migration.\\n2. Deploy the updated compatible application while Q&A remains disabled.\\n3. From the reviewed linked checkout, run `npm run check:migrations`, `supabase migration list`, and `supabase db push --dry-run`.\\n4. Confirm only the expected migration is pending, then apply it with `supabase db push` under separate production approval.\\n5. Verify remote migration version `20261125000000` and approved authenticated quota/RLS smoke checks without invoking OpenAI.\\n6. Set `QA_ENABLED=true` in the hosting provider’s environment settings and redeploy the compatible application with that environment. Verify quota copy and safe errors.\\n\\nIf migration or smoke verification fails, keep Q&A disabled until versions are reconciled. If an automatic deployment pipeline exists or is being implemented, coordinate with Shawn so it cannot activate an application version incompatible with the hosted quota function.\\n\\n## Known limitations and required follow-up\\n\\n- `npm run test:db` could not run locally because Bash is unavailable on the Windows PATH. The GitHub database migration/RLS/concurrency job must pass before merge.\\n- Hosted migration execution, deployment, billing, and production environment changes are intentionally not performed by this PR session.\\n- The SQL/security changes require teammate security review under the project’s AI-assisted development standards.\\n- Do not merge until required CI checks and human reviews pass.\\n\\n## Documentation and AI assistance\\n\\n- Deployment, compatibility, and spending-control documentation: `docs/s3-qa-quota-30.md`\\n- Original Codex transcript: `ai-transcripts/2026-11-25_neel-0064_S3-qa-quota_codex.md`\\n- Review-follow-up Codex transcript: `ai-transcripts/2026-11-25_neel-0064_S3-qa-quota-review-followup_codex.md`\\n\\nCodex assisted with implementation and validation; the developer remains responsible for reviewing every change.\",\"state\":null,\"user\":null,\"assignees\":null,\"labels\":null,\"milestone\":null,\"state_reason\":null,\"comments\":null,\"created_at\":null,\"updated_at\":null,\"closed_at\":null,\"display_url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/131\",\"display_title\":\"Increase per-account Q&A quota from 5 to 30\"},{\"id\":null,\"url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/132\",\"title\":\"S3-002: Rename a saved analysis\",\"issue_number\":132,\"body\":\"Closes #108.\\n\\nAllows users to rename an analysis they own while preserving its identity, source, reviews, ingestion state, and Q&A.\\n- Adds an accessible rename form with validation, cancellation, progress, retry, and success feedback.\\n- Enforces ownership through the authenticated API and Supabase RLS, granting updates to name only.\\n- Rejects unusable names through both the application and direct database writes.\\n- Treats SQL-looking names as literal data and renders HTML-looking names as text.\\nValidation passed:\\n- 112 unit suites: 2,080 tests\\n- 17 production browser tests\\n- Full PostgreSQL security and concurrency suite\\n- Build, lint, typecheck, formatting, and migration checks\\n- 100% changed-line coverage\\nBoth rename migrations are included. The initial migration was applied manually; the follow-up validation policy still needs applying to shared Supabase. \\n\\nAI assistance: OpenAI Codex. Transcript: ai-transcripts/2026-11-25_melissa_S3-002_codex.md.\",\"state\":null,\"user\":null,\"assignees\":null,\"labels\":null,\"milestone\":null,\"state_reason\":null,\"comments\":null,\"created_at\":null,\"updated_at\":null,\"closed_at\":null,\"display_url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/132\",\"display_title\":\"S3-002: Rename a saved analysis\"}]},\"isError\":false}"

do\\r\\n  echo \\\"Applying ${migration}\\\"\\r\\n  psql \\\"${psql_args[@]}\\\" --file \\\"$migration\\\"\\r\\ndone\\r\\n\\r\\nfor test_file in tests/db/*.test.sql; do\\r\\n  echo \\\"Running ${test_file}\\\"\\r\\n  psql \\\"${psql_args[@]}\\\" --file \\\"$test_file\\\"\\r\\ndone\\r\\n\\r\\necho \\\"Running two-session ingestion start-limit regression\\\"\\r\\nbash tests/db/ingestion-start-limit-concurrency.test.sh \\\"$db\\\"\\r\\n\\r\\necho \\\"Running concurrent paid Q&A reservation regression\\\"\\r\\nbash tests/db/review-question-quota-concurrency.test.sh \\\"$db\\\"\\r\\n\\r\\necho \\\"All database tests passed.\\\"\\r\\n---\\r\\ntitle: Route Handlers\\r\\nnav_title: Route Handlers\\r\\ndescription: Create custom request handlers with Next.js Route Handlers using the Web Request and Response APIs.\\r\\nrelated:\\r\\n  title: API Reference\\r\\n  description: Learn more about Route Handlers\\r\\n  links:\\r\\n    - app/api-reference/file-conventions/route\\r\\n    - app/guides/backend-for-frontend\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n## Route Handlers\\r\\n\\r\\nRoute Handlers allow you to create custom request handlers for a given route using the Web [Request](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/Request) and [Response](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/Response) APIs.\\r\\n\\r\\n<Image\\r\\n  alt=\\\"Route.js Special File\\\"\\r\\n  srcLight=\\\"/docs/light/route-special-file.png\\\"\\r\\n  srcDark=\\\"/docs/dark/route-special-file.png\\\"\\r\\n  width=\\\"1600\\\"\\r\\n  height=\\\"444\\\"\\r\\n/>\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Good to know**: Route Handlers are only available inside the `app` directory. They are the equivalent of [API Routes](/docs/pages/building-your-application/routing/api-routes) inside the `pages` directory meaning you **do not** need to use API Routes and Route Handlers together.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Convention\\r\\n\\r\\nRoute Handlers are defined in a [`route.js|ts` file](/docs/app/api-reference/file-conventions/route) inside the `app` directory:\\r\\n\\r\\n```ts filename=\\\"app/api/route.ts\\\" switcher\\r\\nexport async function GET(request: Request) {}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n```js filename=\\\"app/api/route.js\\\" switcher\\r\\nexport async function GET(request) {}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRoute Handlers can be nested anywhere inside the `app` directory, similar to `page.js` and `layout.js`. But there **cannot** be a `route.js` file at the same route segment level as `page.js`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Supported HTTP Methods\\r\\n\\r\\nThe following [HTTP methods](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/HTTP/Methods) are supported: `GET`, `POST`, `PUT`, `PATCH`, `DELETE`, `HEAD`, and `OPTIONS`. If an unsupported method is called, Next.js will return a `405 Method Not Allowed` response.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Extended `NextRequest` and `NextResponse` APIs\\r\\n\\r\\nIn addition to supporting the native [Request](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/Request) and [Response](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/Response) APIs, Next.js extends them with [`NextRequest`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/next-request) and [`NextResponse`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/next-response) to provide convenient helpers for advanced use cases.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Caching\\r\\n\\r\\nRoute Handlers are not cached by default. You can, however, opt into caching for `GET` methods. Other supported HTTP methods are **not** cached. To cache a `GET` method, use a [route config option](/docs/app/guides/caching-without-cache-components#dynamic) such as `export const dynamic = 'force-static'` in your Route Handler file.\\r\\n\\r\\n```ts filename=\\\"app/items/route.ts\\\" switcher\\r\\nexport const dynamic = 'force-static'\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function GET() {\\r\\n  const res = await fetch('https://data.mongodb-api.com/...', {\\r\\n    headers: {\\r\\n      'Content-Type': 'application/json',\\r\\n      'API-Key': process.env.DATA_API_KEY,\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  })\\r\\n  const data = await res.json()\\r\\n\\r\\n  return Response.json({ data })\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n```js filename=\\\"app/items/route.js\\\" switcher\\r\\nexport const dynamic = 'force-static'\\r\\n\\r\\nexport async function GET() {\\r\\n  const res = await fetch('https://data.mongodb-api.com/...', {\\r\\n    headers: {\\r\\n      'Content-Type': 'application/json',\\r\\n      'API-Key': process.env.DATA_API_KEY,\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  })\\r\\n  const data = await res.json()\\r\\n\\r\\n  return Response.json({ data })\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Good to know**: Other supported HTTP methods are **not** cached, even if they are placed alongside a `GET` method that is cached, in the same file.\\r\\n---\\r\\ntitle: useRouter\\r\\ndescription: API reference for the useRouter hook.\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\nThe `useRouter` hook allows you to programmatically change routes inside [Client Components](/docs/app/getting-started/server-and-client-components).\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Recommendation:** Use the [`<Link>` component](/docs/app/api-reference/components/link) for navigation unless you have a specific requirement for using `useRouter`.\\r\\n\\r\\n```tsx filename=\\\"app/example-client-component.tsx\\\" switcher\\r\\n'use client'\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function Page() {\\r\\n  const router = useRouter()\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button type=\\\"button\\\" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>\\r\\n      Dashboard\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  )\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n```jsx filename=\\\"app/example-client-component.js\\\" switcher\\r\\n'use client'\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function Page() {\\r\\n  const router = useRouter()\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <button type=\\\"button\\\" onClick={() => router.push('/dashboard')}>\\r\\n      Dashboard\\r\\n    </button>\\r\\n  )\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## `useRouter()`\\r\\n\\r\\n- `router.push(href: string, { scroll: boolean, transitionTypes: string[] })`: Perform a client-side navigation to the provided route. Adds a new entry into the [browser's history stack](https://developer.mozilla.org/docs/Web/API/History_API). The optional `transitionTypes` are passed to [`React.addTransitionType`](https://react.dev/reference/react/addTransitionType) inside the navigation Transition.\\r\\n- `router.replace(href: string, { scroll: boolean, transitionTypes: string[] })`: Perform a client-side navigation to the provided route without adding a new entry into the browserâ€™s history stack. The optional `transitionTypes` are passed to [`React.addTransitionType`](https://react.dev/reference/react/addTransitionType) inside the navigation Transition.\\r\\n- `router.refresh()`: Refresh the current route. Making a new request to the server, re-fetching data requests, and re-rendering Server Components. The client will merge the updated React Server Component payload without losing unaffected client-side React (e.g. `useState`) or browser state (e.g. scroll position). This clears the [Client Cache](/docs/app/glossary#client-cache) for the current route, but does **not** invalidate the server-side cache. Use [`revalidatePath`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/revalidatePath) or [`revalidateTag`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/revalidateTag) to invalidate server-side cached data.\\r\\n- `router.prefetch(href: string, options?: { onInvalidate?: () => void })`: [Prefetch](/docs/app/getting-started/linking-and-navigating#prefetching) the provided route for faster client-side transitions. The optional `onInvalidate` callback is called when the [prefetched data becomes stale](/docs/app/guides/prefetching#extending-or-ejecting-link).\\r\\n- `router.back()`: Navigate back to the previous route in the browserâ€™s history stack.\\r\\n- `router.forward()`: Navigate forwards to the next page in the browserâ€™s history stack.\\r\\n- `router.bfcacheId`: An opaque string identifier scoped to the current route segment. It changes when the surrounding segment is freshly created by a push or replace navigation, and stays the same for back/forward navigations, `router.refresh()`, and search-param- or hash-only navigations. See [`bfcacheId`](#bfcacheid) below for details.\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Good to know**:\\r\\n>\\r\\n> - You must not send untrusted or unsanitized URLs to `router.push` or `router.replace`, as this can open your site to cross-site scripting (XSS) vulnerabilities. For example, `javascript:` URLs sent to `router.push` or `router.replace` will be executed in the context of your page.\\r\\n> - The `<Link>` component automatically prefetch routes as they become visible in the viewport.\\r\\n> - `refresh()` could re-produce the same result if fetch requests are cached. Other Request-time APIs like `cookies` and `headers` could also change the response.\\r\\n> - The `onInvalidate` callback is called at most once per prefetch request. It signals when you may want to trigger a new prefetch for updated route data.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Migrating from `next/router`\\r\\n\\r\\n- The `useRouter` hook should be imported from `next/navigation` and not `next/router` when using the App Router\\r\\n- The `pathname` string has been removed and is replaced by [`usePathname()`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/use-pathname)\\r\\n- The `query` object has been removed and is replaced by [`useSearchParams()`](/docs/app/api-reference/functions/use-search-params)\\r\\n- `router.events` has been replaced. This bridge adds exactly those,\\r\\n * computed over the SAME `fake.tables` rows the real Q&A context builder reads, so a summary and\\r\\n * a Q&A request in one test always come from one persisted dataset.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Everything else (session verification, target lookups, review reads) still goes through the\\r\\n * unmodified S2-003 fake, so the real production code runs on both paths.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * SOURCE OF TRUTH: the emulations below mirror the SQL in\\r\\n *   supabase/migrations/20261113000000_create_review_summary.sql   (get_review_summary)\\r\\n *   supabase/migrations/20261107030000_bound_ingestion_completions.sql (finish_ingestion_attempt)\\r\\n * whose real behavior is verified against PostgreSQL by tests/db/review-summary.test.sql and\\r\\n * tests/db/ingestion-*.test.sql (`npm run test:db`). Tests using this bridge must assert against\\r\\n * hand-computed constants from the fixtures, never against values this bridge computes.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nconst SUMMARY_RPC = \\\"get_review_summary\\\";\\r\\nconst ATTEMPT_RELATIONS = new Set([\\\"latest_ingestion_attempts\\\", \\\"ingestion_attempts\\\"]);\\r\\n/** Error codes `finish_ingestion_attempt` accepts as terminal for a bound job. */\\r\\nconst TERMINAL_ERROR_CODES = new Set([\\r\\n  \\\"INGESTION_FAILED\\\",\\r\\n  \\\"SOURCE_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED\\\",\\r\\n  \\\"NO_REVIEWS\\\",\\r\\n]);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface BridgeRequest {\\r\\n  readonly resource: string;\\r\\n  readonly actorId: string | undefined;\\r\\n  readonly targetId: string | undefined;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface SummaryQaBridge {\\r\\n  /** Ingestion attempts. Seed with `addAttempt`; `finish` updates them. */\\r\\n  readonly attempts: IngestionAttemptRow[];\\r\\n  /** Requests this bridge served (summary RPC and attempt reads), plus `finish` writes. */\\r\\n  readonly requests: readonly BridgeRequest[];\\r\\n  /** A session-scoped client like `createSupabaseServerClient()`, for direct service calls. */\\r\\n  client(): SupabaseClient;\\r\\n  addAttempt(\\r\\n    overrides: Partial<IngestionAttemptRow> &\\r\\n      Pick<IngestionAttemptRow, \\\"analysis_target_id\\\" | \\\"owner_id\\\">,\\r\\n  ): IngestionAttemptRow;\\r\\n  /** Makes the summary RPC fail with this HTTP status (undefined to clear). */\\r\\n  failSummary(status: number | undefined): void;\\r\\n  /** Emulated attempt writer for `reconcileIngestion`. Only `expireBound` and `finish` are used. */\\r\\n  readonly writer: IngestionDependencies[\\\"writer\\\"];\\r\\n  clearRequests(): void;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst json = (body: unknown, init: ResponseInit = {}) =>\\r\\n  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\\r\\n    ...init,\\r\\n    headers: { \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\", ...(init.headers ?? {}) },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\nconst notEmulated = (name: string) => async (): Promise<never> => {\\r\\n  throw new Error(`summary-qa-bridge does not emulate writer.${name}`);\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function createSummaryQaBridge(fake: FakeSupabase): SummaryQaBridge {\\r\\n  const attempts: IngestionAttemptRow[] = [];\\r\\n  const requests: BridgeRequest[] = [];\\r\\n  let summaryFailure: number | undefined;\\r\\n  let attemptSequence = 0;\\r\\n  let reviewSequence = 0;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const ownsTarget = (actorId: string | undefined, targetId: unknown) =>\\r\\n    actorId !== undefined &&\\r\\n    fake.tables.analysis_targets.some((t) => t.id === targetId && t.owner_id === actorId);\\r\\n\\r\\n  function localFetch(actorId: string | undefined): typeof fetch {\\r\\n    return async (input, init) => {\\r\\n      const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));\\r\\n      const method = (init?.method ?? \\\"GET\\\").toUpperCase();\\r\\n\\r\\n      if (url.pathname === `/rest/v1/rpc/${SUMMARY_RPC}` && method === \\\"POST\\\") {\\r\\n        const targetId = (JSON.parse(String(init?.body ?? \\\"{}\\\")) as { p_target_id?: string })\\r\\n          .p_target_id;\\r\\n        requests.push({ resource: `rpc:${SUMMARY_RPC}`, actorId, targetId });\\r\\n        if (summaryFailure !== undefined) {\\r\\n          return json(\\r\\n            { message: \\\"SUMMARY_BRIDGE_PRIVATE_DB_DIAGNOSTIC\\\" },\\r\\n            { status: summaryFailure },\\r\\n          );\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        // Mirrors: auth.uid() must own the target, else P0002 'Target unavailable'.\\r\\n        if (!ownsTarget(actorId, targetId)) {\\r\\n          return json({ code: \\\"P0002\\\", message: \\\"Target unavailable\\\" }, { status: 404 });\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        // Mirrors: select count(*), avg(rating) from reviews where analysis_target_id = p_target_id.\\r\\n        const ratings = fake.tables.reviews\\r\\n          .filter((row) => row.analysis_target_id === targetId)\\r\\n          .map((row) => Number(row.rating));\\r\\n        return json([\\r\\n          {\\r\\n            review_count: ratings.length,\\r\\n            average_rating: ratings.length\\r\\n              ? ratings.reduce((sum, rating) => sum + rating, 0) / ratings.length\\r\\n              : null,\\r\\n          },\\r\\n        ]);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      const relation = /^\\\\/rest\\\\/v1\\\\/([a-z_]+)$/.exec(url.pathname)?.[1];\\r\\n      if (relation && ATTEMPT_RELATIONS.has(relation) && method === \\\"GET\\\") {\\r\\n        const filters = [...url.searchParams].filter(([, value]) => value.startsWith(\\\"eq.\\\"));\\r\\n        const targetFilter = url.searchParams.get(\\\"analysis_target_id\\\");\\r\\n        requests.push({\\r\\n          resource: relation,\\r\\n          actorId,\\r\\n          targetId: targetFilter?.startsWith(\\\"eq.\\\") ? targetFilter.slice(3) : undefined,\\r\\n        });\\r\\n        let rows = attempts.filter((row) =>\\r\\n          filters.every(\\r\\n            ([key, value]) =>\\r\\n              String((row as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[key]) === value.slice(3),\\r\\n          ),\\r\\n        );\\r\\n        if (relation === \\\"latest_ingestion_attempts\\\") {\\r\\n          // The view keeps only the newest attempt per target.\\r\\n          const newest = new Map<string, IngestionAttemptRow>();\\r\\n          for (const row of rows) {\\r\\n            const seen = newest.get(row.analysis_target_id);\\r\\n            if (!seen || row.started_at > seen.started_at) newest.set(row.analysis_target_id, row);\\r\\n          }\\r\\n          rows = [...newest.values()];\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        const body = structuredClone(rows);\\r\\n        const headers = new Headers(input instanceof Request ? input.headers : init?.headers);\\r\\n        if (/vnd\\\\.pgrst\\\\.object/.test(headers.get(\\\"Accept\\\") ?? \\\"\\\")) {\\r\\n          return body.length === 1\\r\\n            ? json(body[0])\\r\\n            : json({ code: \\\"PGRST116\\\", message: \\\"not one row\\\" }, { status: 406 });\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        return json(body);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n\\r\\n      return json(\\r\\n        { message: `summary-qa-bridge does not serve ${method} ${url.pathname}` },\\r\\n        { status: 404 },\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    };\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const writer: IngestionDependencies[\\\"writer\\\"] = {\\r\\n    reserve: notEmulated(\\\"reserve\\\"),\\r\\n    bind: notEmulated(\\\"bind\\\"),\\r\\n    expireUnbound: notEmulated(\\\"expireUnbound\\\"),\\r\\n    findForRecovery: notEmulated(\\\"findForRecovery\\\"),\\r\\n    async expireBound(attemptId, ownerId) {\\r\\n      const attempt = attempts.find((row) => row.id === attemptId && row.owner_id === ownerId);\\r\\n      if (!attempt) throw new Error(\\\"Target unavailable\\\");\\r\\n      return structuredClone(attempt);\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    // Mirrors finish_ingestion_attempt: owner + target ownership, idempotent after a terminal\\r\\n    // state, only accepted terminal error codes, a failed attempt saves no reviews, and a\\r\\n    // successful one inserts exactly the normalized rows it is given.\\r\\n    async finish(attemptId, ownerId, reviews, skippedCount, identity, errorCode) {\\r\\n      const attempt = attempts.find(\\r\\n        (row) =>\\r\\n          row.id === attemptId &&\\r\\n          row.owner_id === ownerId &&\\r\\n          ownsTarget(ownerId, row.analysis_target_id),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      requests.push({\\r\\n        resource: \\\"rpc:finish_ingestion_attempt\\\",\\r\\n        actorId: ownerId,\\r\\n        targetId: attempt?.analysis_target_id,\\r\\n      });\\r\\n      if (!attempt) throw new Error(\\\"Target unavailable\\\");\\r\\n      if (attempt.status !== \\\"processing\\\") return structuredClone(attempt);\\r\\n      if (\\r\\n        attempt.provider_request_id !== null &&\\r\\n        errorCode !== null &&\\r\\n        !TERMINAL_ERROR_CODES.has(errorCode)\\r\\n      ) {\\r\\n        return structuredClone(attempt);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (skippedCount < 0) throw new Error(\\\"Invalid completion\\\");\\r\\n      if (errorCode === null) {\\r\\n        if (attempt.provider_request_id === null || reviews.length === 0 || identity === null) {\\r\\n          throw new Error(\\\"Missing verified completion\\\");\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        for (const review of reviews) {\\r\\n          reviewSequence += 1;\\r\\n          const n = String(reviewSequence).padStart(12, \\\"0\\\");\\r\\n          const row: ReviewRow = {\\r\\n            id: `3f000000-0000-4000-8000-${n}`,\\r\\n            analysis_target_id: attempt.analysis_target_id,\\r\\n            review_text: String(review.text),\\r\\n            rating: Number(review.rating),\\r\\n            source_review_id: (review.source_review_id as string | null) ?? null,\\r\\n            reviewer_name: (review.reviewer_name as string | null) ?? null,\\r\\n as Record<string, unknown> | null) ?? null,\\r\\n            created_at: `2026-11-15T10:00:${String(reviewSequence % 60).padStart(2, \\\"0\\\")}.000Z`,\\r\\n          };\\r\\n          fake.tables.reviews.push(row);\\r\\n        }\\r\\n      } else if (reviews.length !== 0) {\\r\\n        throw new Error(\\\"Failed attempt cannot save reviews\\\");\\r\\n      }\\r\\n  owner_id: \\\"user-a\\\",\\r\\n  state: \\\"not_ingested\\\",\\r\\n  started_at: null,\\r\\n  finished_at: null,\\r\\n  saved_count: 0,\\r\\n  skipped_count: 0,\\r\\n  error_code: null,\\r\\n};\\r\\nit(\\\"uses explicit owner, target filters and backend no-attempt confirmation\\\", async () => {\\r\\n  const db = database([row]);\\r\\n  const result = await getTargetIngestionSummaries(db.client, expect(result.get(\\\"target-a\\\")?.state).toBe(\\\"not_ingested\\\");\\r\\n});\\r\\nit(\\\"does not invent no-ingestion for missing or truncated rows\\\", async () => {\\r\\n  const db = Baseline: `3538ea9f` (main, including S3-013). Author: Shawn.\\n+\\n+## Behavior and persistence decision\\n+\\n+The owned workspace offers **Delete analysis**. A native modal names the analysis, explains\\n+irreversibility and audit retention, and initially focuses Cancel. Cancel and Escape do not send\\n+a request. Confirmation makes one DELETE request; failures keep the dialog available for retry\\n+and show a fixed safe message. Successful deletion makes a full navigation to My Targets so\\n+Next's preserved workspaces, answers and sidebar state are discarded. Browser-history access\\n+to the deleted URL must still pass the server ownership check and cannot retrieve its data.\\n+\\n+Proposed S3-BR-008 persistence design, subject to normal and independent security review:\\n+\\n+| Data | Deletion behavior |\\n+| --- | --- |\\n+| Owned `analysis_targets` row | Permanently deleted by a narrow authenticated RPC |\\n+| Associated `reviews` | Deleted by the existing `ON DELETE CASCADE` FK |\\n+| `ingestion_attempts` | Retained for billing/safety; FK becomes null through `ON DELETE SET NULL` |\\n+| Paid Q&A reservations | Retained; account-scoped, already independent of the target |\\n+| Transient Q&A / sidebar state | Discarded by full navigation after success |\\n+| Future persisted Q&A history | Not on this baseline; history owner must add a target FK with cascading deletion or an explicitly reviewed alternative |\\n+\\n+Retained ingestion records include existing source/job snapshots; this is **not** an erasure of\\n+every historical audit field. Existing RLS requires a live owned parent, so detached audits are\\n+not readable by browser sessions. They still count toward the existing five-start rolling quota.\\n+Deleting and recreating targets cannot refund ingestion or Q&A spending reservations. Account\\n+deletion retains its existing owner-FK cascades.\\n+\\n+## Security boundaries\\n+\\n+- **S3-BR-007:** The DELETE handler verifies the current session and runs the existing owner-scoped\\n+  lookup before attempting deletion. Missing, malformed and foreign IDs return the same 404.\\n+- The body and query parameters cannot supply ownership or choose a different target.\\n+- Cross-origin browser mutations are rejected; DELETE is not supported by ordinary HTML forms.\\n+- The session-scoped client calls `delete_owned_analysis(p_target_id)`; no service-role client or\\n+  client-supplied owner is used. The database derives the owner from `auth.uid()` and checks it\\n+  inside the DELETE statement, closing the lookup/write race.\\n+- Direct table DELETE remains revoked. The RPC is granted only to authenticated sessions and\\n+  fails without `auth.uid()`. It uses an empty search path and qualified table/function names.\\n+- The target delete, review cascade and audit detachment are one database transaction.\\n+- Detached attempts are unavailable to callback recovery. The database lifecycle functions also\\n+  require a live owned target and reject late binding/completion after deletion.\\n+- Responses are private/no-store; failures/logs do not include database diagnostics, user input\\n+  or credentials. Target names remain escaped React text.\\n+- Duplicate clicks are guarded synchronously. Hidden/unmounted workspaces abort their request\\n+  and ignore late success, so old deletion responses cannot navigate a newly active workspace.\\n+  Aborting a browser request does not promise to cancel a database mutation already committed.\\n+\\n+## Integration handoff\\n+\\n+- **Melissa / S3-002 (#132):** Both stories add a workspace management action and a parent target\\n+  route. Merge both PATCH and DELETE exports; preserve both Rename and Delete actions in the\\n+  PageHeader action area. Do not replace either feature with the other.\\n+- **Deep / refresh:** `ingestion_attempts.analysis_target_id` is nullable only for retained audit\\n+  rows after parent deletion. The row type and callback read handle this explicitly. Preserve\\n+  parent ownership checks on refresh/bind/completion and never promote a detached attempt.\\n+- **Aryan / Save As and history:** Copies need their own review rows/identity so deleting one\\n+  cannot remove the other's data. New history tables must define their target FK deletion policy.\\n+- Migration version `20261126000000` is independent of the existing quota/rename version collision.\\n+  Recheck all pending versions and remote migration history before deployment.\\n+\\n+## Deployment and acceptance\\n+\\n+No hosted migration or provider configuration is changed by this work. Deploy the reviewed\\n+migration before exposing the delete UI; the old app tolerates the schema change because live\\n+target attempts remain attached. A new app against an unmigrated database fails safely when the\\n+RPC is unavailable. Do not restore raw table DELETE privileges as a workaround.\\n+\\n+Required human checks before final acceptance:\\n+\\n+1. Apply only the reviewed pending migration through the team's authorized deployment process.\\n+2. With disposable owned data, Cancel, then delete an analysis with saved reviews; verify it is\\n+   absent after reload and direct retrieval is denied. Do not test on valuable shared data.\\n+3. With another account, verify deletion is denied and both datasets remain intact.\\n+4. Inspect retained audit/quota behavior using approved read-only checks; do not start paid jobs.\\n+5. Obtain normal and independent authorization/database security review, passing CI (including\\n+   PostgreSQL and concurrency jobs), and final author/manual acceptance before closing #111.\\n+\\n+## Verification record\\n+\\n+Local results will be finalized after the full checks finish. Initial API/UI tests: 23 passed.\\n+All 10 migrations and all 13 SQL assertion files passed in a disposable PGlite 0.6.0 database,\\n+including real roles, RLS, functions, cascade and audit-retention assertions. This is local WASM\\n+PostgreSQL execution, not hosted Supabase acceptance or the two-session shell concurrency runner.\\n+\\n+Two in-memory SQL mutations were caught independently: removing the RPC owner predicate fails\\n+the foreign-user assertion; replacing audit detachment with cascade fails the retained-budget\\n+assertion. Neither mutation was written into the repository migration.\\n+\\n+No paid provider calls, real credentials or shared Supabase writes were used. PGlite was installed\\n+in a temporary tools directory with install scripts disabled; it is not a project dependency.\\n+\\n+AI assistance: OpenAI Codex. The complete available task dialogue/tool records are retained in\\n+`ai-transcripts/2026-11-26_shawn_S3-005_codex.md`, with privacy redactions and a stated cutoff.\\n*** End Patch\"));\ntext(await tools.write_stdin({session_id:65883,chars:\"\",yield_time_ms:1000,max_output_tokens:2200}));\n",
  "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {

}

Existing RLS requires a live owned parent, so detached audits are\\r\\nnot readable by browser sessions. They still count toward the existing five-start rolling quota.\\r\\nDeleting and recreating targets cannot refund ingestion or Q&A spending reservations. ## 175. 2026-11-26T06:21:34.918Z — custom_tool_call / exec

```json
{
  "type": "custom_tool_call",
  "id": "ctc_02d5b8b62e65bf06016b07cfd458a887d19e77df9f0793d7bd",
  "status": "completed",
  "call_id": "call_807ae09d55f140248139fc786f527d25",
  "name": "exec",
  "input": "const repo=\"aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six\";\nconst sha=\"0cc56adbe7126f6cc2b1d3c14569516628e683a2\";\nconst body=`Refs [#111](https://github.com/${repo}/issues/111)\n\n## Outcome\n\nUsers can delete an analysis they own from its workspace after an explicit confirmation naming the analysis. Successful deletion returns to My Targets and removes the analysis and its saved reviews from normal retrieval.\n\nCommit: [\\`0cc56adb\\`](https://github.com/${repo}/commit/${sha}). Baseline: main \\`3538ea9f\\`, including S3-013.\n\n## Changes\n\n- Add a destructive **Delete analysis** action and a native modal with Cancel initially focused, Escape/cancel behavior, progress, duplicate-submit protection and safe retry feedback.\n- Add authenticated \\`DELETE /api/analysis-targets/[targetId]\\`. Ownership is checked before deletion; body/query fields cannot override the user or target. Missing, foreign and malformed targets receive the same 404.\n- Call a session-scoped \\`delete_owned_analysis(p_target_id)\\` RPC. It derives ownership from \\`auth.uid()\\` and enforces it inside the DELETE statement, independently of the earlier application lookup.\n- Keep direct table DELETE revoked. The RPC uses an empty search path and authenticated-only execution.\n- Discard cached workspace/sidebar/Q&A state through a full navigation after verified success; hidden/unmounted workspaces ignore late deletion responses.\n- Preserve fixed safe errors, private/no-store responses, escaped target names and same-host browser Origin checks compatible with forwarded hosting.\n- Add API, component, production-browser and SQL regressions, documentation and the authentic available Codex transcript.\n\n## Associated data and spending safeguards — proposed design for review\n\nMigration: \\`20261126000000_delete_owned_analysis.sql\\`.\n\n| Record | Behavior |\n| --- | --- |\n| AnalysisTarget | Permanently deleted |\n| Saved reviews | Existing FK cascades the deletion |\n| Ingestion attempts | Retained; target FK changes to nullable / ON DELETE SET NULL |\n| Account Q&A reservations | Retained, unchanged |\n| Transient workspace state | Discarded after successful deletion |\n\nIngestion audits include existing source/job snapshots; this does not erase every historical audit field. Existing RLS hides detached audits from browser sessions, while their reservations still count toward the paid-ingestion limit. Deleting/recreating analyses cannot refund usage.\n\nThe nullable audit-row type, callback recovery read and shared summary/Q&A test helper are updated accordingly. Detached attempts cannot be recovered through the application; existing database lifecycle RPCs also require a live owned parent and reject late binding/completion.\n\nThis retention design needs team/security approval under S3-BR-008. S3-BR-007 is enforced at both API and database boundaries.\n\n## Verification\n\nAll validation used synthetic data, local fixture services or disposable databases. No paid provider calls or hosted Supabase writes were made.\n\n| Check | Result |\n| --- | --- |\n| Focused deletion/API/audit tests | 48 passed |\n| Full Jest coverage | 112 suites / 2,086 tests passed; no skips |\n| Overall line coverage | 96.17% |\n| Changed-code coverage gate | Passed: 230/230 source lines; 73/74 branch outcomes |\n| Production build, explicit typecheck, lint, formatting, whitespace and migration-version validation | Passed |\n| New deletion production-browser scenario | Passed |\n| Full production auth/workspace browser suite | **42 passed / 2 known baseline failures** |\n| SQL migrations/assertions | All 10 migrations and 13 SQL test files passed in disposable PGlite 0.6.0 |\n\nThe browser scenario covers keyboard confirmation/cancellation, mobile layout, server failure/retry, two accounts, foreign deletion denial, actual owner deletion, empty sidebar/list, reload and direct retrieval denial afterward. Mobile/desktop screenshots remain in ignored local test artifacts.\n\nIndependent in-memory SQL mutations were caught: removing the RPC ownership predicate fails the foreign-user assertion; cascading ingestion audits instead of retaining them fails the budget-retention assertion.\n\nPGlite is a temporary validation tool, not a project dependency. This does not replace the normal CI PostgreSQL/two-session concurrency job or hosted acceptance.\n\n### Known baseline browser failures\n\n\\`tests/e2e/auth/analysis-targets.spec.ts:99\\` and \\`:177\\` still expect the old \\`Target created. You can now start ingestion.\\` banner, although the created workspace opens successfully. Both were independently reproduced on unchanged main \\`24f083fb\\` during S3-014 verification. The creation form/notice and those tests are unchanged through baseline \\`3538ea9f\\` and are not changed here.\n\nThe complete browser suite is not reported as green. Those failures remain tracked separately.\n\n## Integration and deployment\n\n- **S3-002 / #132:** Both stories touch the parent target route and workspace management actions. Preserve both PATCH/DELETE exports and Rename/Delete controls when reconciling; do not overwrite either feature.\n- **Refresh:** Detached audit rows have no active target. Preserve parent checks during binding/completion and never promote a detached attempt.\n- **Save As/history:** Copies require independent owned data. Persisted Q&A history is not present on this baseline; new history tables must define a cascading target FK or an explicitly reviewed alternative.\n- Deploy the reviewed migration before exposing the new delete UI. An unavailable RPC fails safely; do not restore raw DELETE grants as a workaround.\n- Recheck pending migration versions and actual remote migration history before applying anything. No migration, provider or production configuration has been applied by this PR.\n\n## Review and remaining acceptance\n\n**Draft intentionally.** Keep #111 open until normal review, independent authorization/database security review, current-head CI, approved retention/integration design, authorized migration deployment and author/manual acceptance are complete.\n\nReview especially: atomic ownership enforcement, RPC privileges/search path, audit/quota retention, late callback denial, browser Origin/proxy handling, and shared rename/refresh/history integration.\n\nNo dependency, authentication-provider, OpenAI or Outscraper configuration change. Do not merge automatically.\n\n## Evidence and AI assistance\n\nAI assistance: OpenAI Codex. No sub-agents were used.\n\n- [Implementation, persistence policy, test evidence and handoff](https://github.com/${repo}/blob/${sha}/docs/s3-005-delete-owned-analysis.md)\n- [Original available task dialogue and tool records](https://github.com/${repo}/blob/${sha}/ai-transcripts/2026-11-26_shawn_S3-005_codex.md)\n\nThe transcript includes failures/corrections, privacy redactions, a disposition note and an explicit pre-commit cutoff; it is not a reconstructed summary.\n`;\nstore(\"s3005PrBody\",body);\nconst result=await tools.mcp__codex_apps__github_create_pull_request({repo_full_name:repo,title:\"S3-005: Delete owned analyses while retaining usage audits\",head:\"feature/S3-005-delete-owned-analysis\",base:\"main\",body,draft:true});\ntext(result);\nstore(\"s3005PrCreated\",result);\n",
  "internal_chat_message_metadata_passthrough": {
    "turn_id": "01a21682-2062-7ba0-8c14-dff30ac20ad2",
    "create_time": 1795674069.0213609
  }
}

      "text": "{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Action completed.\"}],\"structuredContent\":{\"url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/133\",\"number\":133,\"state\":\"open\",\"merged\":false,\"mergeable\":true,\"draft\":false,\"body\":\"Refs [#120](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/issues/120)\\n\\n## Outcome\\n\\nUsers can inspect every review cited by a grounded answer beyond the short inline evidence list. The UI explicitly distinguishes this cited subset from the complete saved review dataset.\\n\\nReviewLens currently uses bounded complete-dataset context rather than question-specific retrieval. For S3-014, “supporting reviews” therefore means the unique reviews cited by the validated answer; this does not claim to discover every potentially relevant review.\\n\\n## Changes\\n\\n- Show up to three numbered supporting reviews inline.\\n- For larger cited sets, provide a native **View all N supporting reviews** disclosure containing the full cited set in citation order. The full list intentionally repeats the inline preview and explains that behavior.\\n- Preserve every citation's numbering, persisted text/rating/date and safe unavailable-record fallback.\\n- Reuse active-target citation resolution and escaped text rendering; opening the disclosure performs no network request or paid model call.\\n- Add component/integration coverage for membership, ordering, unavailable records, safe markup, account/target changes, preserved-page cleanup and subsequent questions.\\n- Add a dedicated synthetic browser account with seven saved fixture reviews, of which six are cited. Existing fixture accounts' counts remain unchanged.\\n- Update UI standards and retain the original available Codex dialogue/tool records with privacy redactions and an explicit cutoff.\\n\\nNo API, database schema, migration, dependency, model, prompt, quota, production configuration or review-selection contract changed.\\n\\n## Requirements and boundaries\\n\\n- **S3-BR-020:** Users can inspect the full cited set when it exceeds the inline preview.\\n- **S3-BR-021:** Existing server authorization and active-target resolution remain authoritative. Foreign-target evidence is unavailable, never fetched through a broader fallback.\\n- **S3-BR-022:** Evidence fields come from stored Review objects; unavailable data is not invented; markup remains inert.\\n- **S3-BR-023:** The cited subset complements the complete review browser already merged under S3-015.\\n- **S3-BR-024:** Expansion remains scoped to the active target and does not query or mutate the persisted dataset.\\n\\nThis is a read-only presentation change, not a replacement for backend ownership/RLS or semantic-grounding evaluation.\\n\\n## Verification\\n\\nBaseline: main `24f083fb`. Node 24.21.0; Chromium; local fixture services only.\\n\\n| Check | Actual result |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| Focused component/integration and inherited citation tests | 24/24 passed |\\n| Full Jest with coverage | 112 suites / 2,052 tests passed; no skips |\\n| Changed-line coverage | 100% — 54/54 source lines; 4/4 changed branch outcomes |\\n| Overall line/statement coverage | 96.10% |\\n| Formatting, lint, explicit typecheck, migration filename checks | Passed |\\n| Production build | Passed; 12/12 static pages |\\n| New supporting-review browser scenario | Passed in development and production |\\n| Keyboard / responsive checks | Enter/Space open/close, 375px layout, full-set membership and reload cleanup passed |\\n| Full production authentication browser suite | **42 passed / 2 failed**, detailed below |\\n\\nMutation checks caught all three deliberate faults: truncated expanded evidence (5 test failures), missing explorer (9 failures), and removed active-target resolution (1 failure). All temporary mutations were restored.\\n\\nValidation used isolated checkouts without the user's `.env.local`, disabled paid features and a recording/mocked model boundary. A temporary Turbopack root setting accommodated linked dependencies in validation and was restored; it is not in this PR. No live Supabase, OpenAI or Outscraper acceptance is claimed.\\n\\n### Known baseline browser failures\\n\\nTwo existing scenarios in `tests/e2e/auth/analysis-targets.spec.ts` (lines 99 and 177) expect the old `Target created. You can now start ingestion.` banner. Snapshots show the created workspace opened successfully instead.\\n\\n**Both failures were independently reproduced on a separate unchanged-main `24f083fb` production build without this PR's source or fixture changes.** They predate S3-014. Their assertions were not weakened, skipped or repaired in this story. The full browser suite is not being reported as green.\\n\\n## Integration\\n\\n- S3-015 / #129 is already included in the baseline.\\n- S3-013 / #127 changes `ReviewQuestions.tsx` and `citations.ts`; this PR leaves those files and its changed tests alone.\\n- The 11 new tests passed in a temporary combination with S3-013 `fa106e14` and main. Its later `70b026c` follow-up changes test synchronization/transcript only.\\n- Recheck against S3-013's final approved head. Its stricter parser also requires remaining legacy browser stubs to supply valid citations; the new S3-014 browser response already does.\\n- Coordinate future filter/history work so browsing filters never silently broaden or replace an answer's cited evidence.\\n\\n## Manual verification\\n\\n1. Open an owned analysis with saved reviews and obtain a grounded answer.\\n2. For more than three citations, open **View all N supporting reviews** and inspect the later cited records and unchanged numbering.\\n3. Confirm these are labeled as cited evidence, not the entire dataset. For three or fewer citations, all are already inline.\\n4. Switch account/target, submit another question, or obtain a non-answer: old evidence must disappear.\\n5. Use the deterministic browser test for a guaranteed six-citation demo; a live model is not guaranteed to cite more than three reviews.\\n\\n```powershell\\nnpx.cmd playwright test --config=playwright.auth.config.ts tests/e2e/auth/supporting-reviews.spec.ts\\n```\\n\\nDesktop/mobile screenshots are retained locally in the isolated validation checkout's ignored `test-results` folder; no screenshots, traces, credentials or generated test artifacts are committed.\\n\\n## Review and remaining acceptance\\n\\nDraft intentionally: current-head CI and independent functional/security review are pending; the known baseline browser failures must stay tracked. Final applicable public-deployment/manual acceptance is not yet recorded. Keep issue #120 open until final acceptance and merge. Do not merge automatically or bypass protected-branch checks.\\n\\n## Evidence and AI assistance\\n\\nAI assistance: **OpenAI Codex**. No sub-agents were used.\\n\\n- [Implementation, canonical-rule mapping and test evidence](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/blob/1dfe31a5d68b3eed25c80df4131d5c569242a68f/docs/s3-014-supporting-reviews.md)\\n- [Original available task and follow-up transcript](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/blob/1dfe31a5d68b3eed25c80df4131d5c569242a68f/ai-transcripts/2026-11-25_shawn_S3-014_codex.md)\\n\",\"title\":\"S3-014: Explore the complete cited review set\",\"base\":\"main\",\"base_sha\":\"24f083fb5e35efe198930a48295122b110599ed5\",\"head\":\"feature/S3-014-supporting-reviews\",\"head_sha\":\"1dfe31a5d68b3eed25c80df4131d5c569242a68f\",\"head_repo_id\":1436385791,\"head_repo_full_name\":\"aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six\",\"merge_commit_sha\":\"7ded893c98d397e20bbb19a9ddc8fe0b7f1cc728\",\"user\":{\"login\":\"shawnd121\",\"name\":\"shawnd121\",\"email\":null,\"avatar_url\":\"https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/142047263?v=4\",\"id\":142047263},\"requested_reviewers\":null,\"requested_team_reviewers\":null,\"diff\":null,\"comments\":null,\"created_at\":\"2026-11-26T01:49:51Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2026-11-26T15:30:26Z\",\"closed_at\":null,\"merged_at\":null,\"commits\":1,\"changed_files\":8,\"additions\":1519,\"deletions\":26,\"display_url\":\"https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/pull/133\",\"display_title\":\"S3-014: Explore the complete cited review set\"},\"isError\":false}"

Successful deletion returns to My Targets and removes the analysis and its saved reviews from normal retrieval.\\n\\nCommit: [`0cc56adb`](https://github.com/aum-2004/CS490_Review_Lens_AI_Fantastic_Six/commit/0cc56adbe7126f6cc2b1d3c14569516628e683a2). Baseline: main `3538ea9f`, including S3-013.\\n\\n## Changes\\n\\n- Add a destructive **Delete analysis** action and a native modal with Cancel initially focused, Escape/cancel behavior, progress, duplicate-submit protection and safe retry feedback.\\n- Add authenticated `DELETE /api/analysis-targets/[targetId]`. Ownership is checked before deletion; body/query fields cannot override the user or target. Missing, foreign and malformed targets receive the same 404.\\n- Call a session-scoped `delete_owned_analysis(p_target_id)` RPC. It derives ownership from `auth.uid()` and enforces it inside the DELETE statement, independently of the earlier application lookup.\\n- Keep direct table DELETE revoked. The RPC uses an empty search path and authenticated-only execution.\\n- Discard cached workspace/sidebar/Q&A state through a full navigation after verified success; hidden/unmounted workspaces ignore late deletion responses.\\n- Preserve fixed safe errors, private/no-store responses, escaped target names and same-host browser Origin checks compatible with forwarded hosting.\\n- Add API, component, production-browser and SQL regressions, documentation and the authentic available Codex transcript.\\n\\n## Associated data and spending safeguards — proposed design for review\\n\\nMigration: `20261126000000_delete_owned_analysis.sql`.\\n\\n| Record | Behavior |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| AnalysisTarget | Permanently deleted |\\n| Saved reviews | Existing FK cascades the deletion |\\n| Ingestion attempts | Retained; target FK changes to nullable / ON DELETE SET NULL |\\n| Account Q&A reservations | Retained, unchanged |\\n| Transient workspace state | Discarded after successful deletion |\\n\\nIngestion audits include existing source/job snapshots; this does not erase every historical audit field. Existing RLS hides detached audits from browser sessions, while their reservations still count toward the paid-ingestion limit. Deleting/recreating analyses cannot refund usage.\\n\\nThe nullable audit-row type, callback recovery read and shared summary/Q&A test helper are updated accordingly. Detached attempts cannot be recovered through the application; existing database lifecycle RPCs also require a live owned parent and reject late binding/completion.\\n\\nThis retention design needs team/security approval under S3-BR-008. S3-BR-007 is enforced at both API and database boundaries.\\n\\n## Verification\\n\\nAll validation used synthetic data, local fixture services or disposable databases. No paid provider calls or hosted Supabase writes were made.\\n\\n| Check | Result |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| Focused deletion/API/audit tests | 48 passed |\\n| Full Jest coverage | 112 suites / 2,086 tests passed; no skips |\\n| Overall line coverage | 96.17% |\\n| Changed-code coverage gate | Passed: 230/230 source lines; 73/74 branch outcomes |\\n| Production build, explicit typecheck, lint, formatting, whitespace and migration-version validation | Passed |\\n| New deletion production-browser scenario | Passed |\\n| Full production auth/workspace browser suite | **42 passed / 2 known baseline failures** |\\n| SQL migrations/assertions | All 10 migrations and 13 SQL test files passed in disposable PGlite 0.6.0 |\\n\\nThe browser scenario covers keyboard confirmation/cancellation, mobile layout, server failure/retry, two accounts, foreign deletion denial, actual owner deletion, empty sidebar/list, reload and direct retrieval denial afterward. Mobile/desktop screenshots remain in ignored local test artifacts.\\n\\nIndependent in-memory SQL mutations were caught: removing the RPC ownership predicate fails the foreign-user assertion; cascading ingestion audits instead of retaining them fails the budget-retention assertion.\\n\\nPGlite is a temporary validation tool, not a project dependency. This does not replace the normal CI PostgreSQL/two-session concurrency job or hosted acceptance.\\n\\n### Known baseline browser failures\\n\\n`tests/e2e/auth/analysis-targets.spec.ts:99` and `:177` still expect the old `Target created. You can now start ingestion.` banner, although the created workspace opens successfully. Both were independently reproduced on unchanged main `24f083fb` during S3-014 verification. The creation form/notice and those tests are unchanged through baseline `3538ea9f` and are not changed here.\\n\\nThe complete browser suite is not reported as green. Those failures remain tracked separately.\\n\\n## Integration and deployment\\n\\n- **S3-002 / #132:** Both stories touch the parent target route and workspace management actions. Preserve both PATCH/DELETE exports and Rename/Delete controls when reconciling; do not overwrite either feature.\\n- **Refresh:** Detached audit rows have no active target. Preserve parent checks during binding/completion and never promote a detached attempt.\\n- **Save As/history:** Copies require independent owned data. Persisted Q&A history is not present on this baseline; new history tables must define a cascading target FK or an explicitly reviewed alternative.\\n- Deploy the reviewed migration before exposing the new delete UI. ## Implementation disposition (human review pending)

Implemented: confirmed owned deletion, transactional review cascade, retained ingestion audit/quota records, and unavailable orphan callback recovery. Changed: the internal-Origin comparison after browser rejection, nullable audit handling in the shared helper, and browser assertions around full navigation/empty sidebar. Rejected: cascading ingestion audits because it would refund paid-start limits. Pending: team review of retention/integration, independent security review, remote CI, authorized migration deployment and human/manual acceptance. Synthetic screenshot binary is excluded; visible text and tool errors/corrections are retained.
